/* Overlays: Dialog, Sheet, AlertDialog. React Aria portals them to the end
   of the document, so they take the document's tokens. The scrim mixes
   the darkest neutral into transparency, which darkens the page in both
   themes; there is no semantic scrim token yet. Widths are multiples of
   the space scale, as there are no dimension tokens. The z-index lifts the
   overlay over the raised options of a choice group. */

   page can still restyle one scrollbar. */

| `--stoa-space-4` | space | primitive | `16px` | - | (css) .stoa-shortcuts__list, (css) /* ==== Overlays, lists and content ==== */

/* Overlays: Dialog, Sheet, AlertDialog. React Aria portals them to the end
   of the document, so they take the document's tokens. The scrim mixes
   the darkest neutral into transparency, which darkens the page in both
   themes; there is no semantic scrim token yet. Widths are multiples of
   the space scale, as there are no dimension tokens. The z-index lifts the
   overlay over the raised options of a choice group. */
.stoa-overlay, AlertDialog, AppHeader, Callout, Checkbox, DescriptionList, EmptyState, EventStrip, FilterBar, LineChart, PageShell, ScatterChart, ShortcutList, StatBar, Switch, Tabs, ToastRegion |
